v0.8.0 GitHub ★ 26
Example 1 of 8 · real output · sbomlyze v0.8.0

Same tag, different image

nginx:1.28.0 built in April and in December 2025 both run nginx 1.28.0, but 40 Debian packages differ, including the OpenSSL fix for CVE-2025-9230.

containerdeb Scanned-image changeOS release changeDebian version orderingCoverage warningAll output formats

What happened

Docker’s official nginx:1.28.0 tag was pushed again many times. Docker’s public repo-info history for this one tag lists 20 different multi-platform digests and 12 different linux/amd64 images between April and December 2025 (extracted in the tag-history evidence file below). These are the first and the last linux/amd64 builds, built on 23 April and 8 December 2025.

Both print nginx version: nginx/1.28.0 when run. sbomlyze shows what the tag hides: the Debian base moved from 12.10 to 12.12 and 40 packages changed version, all of them upgrades. 36 keep their upstream version and change only the Debian revision, which is how Debian ships fixes to a stable release. At every release, the package changes are checked against dpkg-query output captured inside both images: they must match exactly.

One of them is OpenSSL 3.0.15-1~deb12u1 → 3.0.17-1~deb12u3. Debian fixed CVE-2025-9230 in 3.0.17-1~deb12u3 (DSA-6015-1). A server still running the April image has nginx 1.28.0 and the unpatched OpenSSL.

Syft recorded a package digest for only 1 of the 110 packages that kept their version, and sbomlyze says so instead of reporting “no integrity drift”. It compared installed files instead: 960 changed, all in upgraded packages or in no package at all; no package that kept its version had a file change.

What this does not prove. This does not show that nginx is exploitable. It shows that the image still behind the same tag in April lacks the patched OpenSSL package. Results are for linux/amd64.

Inputs

Generated once with Syft 1.56.0, linux/amd64, and kept unchanged in testdata/showcase in the repository. Every sbomlyze release re-runs the commands below on these exact files.

nginx:1.28.0, built 23 Apr 2025
syft scan registry:index.docker.io/library/nginx@sha256:7c46bce526d9abd904d656b4afe3742be3fffd9bc2d361dc4ba68bcbc22ca301 -o syft-json=nginx-1.28.0-apr.syft.json
syft scan registry:index.docker.io/library/nginx@sha256:7c46bce526d9abd904d656b4afe3742be3fffd9bc2d361dc4ba68bcbc22ca301 -o cyclonedx-json=nginx-1.28.0-apr.cdx.json
syft scan registry:index.docker.io/library/nginx@sha256:7c46bce526d9abd904d656b4afe3742be3fffd9bc2d361dc4ba68bcbc22ca301 -o spdx-json=nginx-1.28.0-apr.spdx.json
